f﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿inding.

Q﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿UESTIONS.md.

n﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍﻿ew evidence.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍﻿0.2 Retirement Procedure
W﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿hen a documented limitation is fixed, the four-place pattern reverses:
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿. Delete the fixture file.
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍﻿. Delete the pinning test.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿. Remove the README "Remaining limitations" entry.
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Add a "Retired in vX.Y.Z" entry to CHANGELOG explaining the promotion.
R﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿etirement is itself a discipline. A documented limitation is a deferred fix, not an accepted
s﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿hortcoming. The retirement ledger (Section 17) tracks every retirement and is the empirical
e﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿vidence that the framework reduces structural debt over time rather than accumulating it.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿1. The Four-Place Documentation Pattern
A﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ documented limitation has four canonical surfaces. All four must agree:
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. CHANGELOG entry explaining what was deferred and why.
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿. README "Remaining limitations" section with a user-facing bullet.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿. Fixture file with a reproducer and docstring explaining the deliberate behavior.
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿. Pinning test that asserts the current behavior, ensuring any change is intentional.

C﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿C BY-NC 4.0 · Bilal Syed Arfeen · 18 of 48

F﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿igure 6. The four-place documentation pattern.
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿he discipline is that a documented limitation is a contract: the team promises the behavior will
n﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿ot silently change. Without all four surfaces, the contract is not enforceable. The pinning test in
p﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿articular is the structural backbone: if the behavior is changed without intent, the pinning test fails
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿nd the change is surfaced before merge.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿2. Pre-Flight Self-Review and the Four-Revision Prompt Review
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿his section is the highest-leverage part of this document for reducing reviewer friction. Pre-flight
d﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿iscipline catches the failures that would otherwise reach the auditor; the four-revision prompt
r﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿eview catches the failures that would otherwise reach the executor.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍﻿2.1 Ten-Item Pre-Flight Checklist
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Are there hedges of the form "(if it still exists)"? Replace each with a specific instruction.
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. For each symbol removal, identify every import of that symbol.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. For each new subprocess or network test, identify failure modes and mark appropriately.
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. For each pip install or shell command in a test, pin the working directory.
5﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. For each version-pinned URL, verify it targets a specific tag, not a moving reference.
6﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. For each "test_X_present" test, ask whether functional behavior is also tested.
7﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. State the expected test count after the change.
8﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿. Run the em-dash check against all new content.
9﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿. Replace date placeholders with actual dates at commit time.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿0. For each function behavior change, identify whether the docstring also changes.

C﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿C BY-NC 4.0 · Bilal Syed Arfeen · 19 of 48

1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿2.2 The Five Questions
B﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿efore submitting any release for review, answer in writing:
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿. What is the smallest input that demonstrates this fix is working?
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. What is the smallest input that would have demonstrated the bug before the fix?
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿. What does this fix not do that a reader might expect it to?
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿. What new code paths does this introduce, and what are their boundaries?
5﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿. What documented limitations does this retire or introduce?
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿2.3 The Four-Revision Prompt Review

a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿nd is itself a finding.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿3. Anti-Patterns

"﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿All tests pass, ship it." Tests reflect what the team thought to test. An audit finds what they did
not.
